102: def _parse_files_pipe_payload(paths_str):
103:     """FILES: [paste_id|]path|path — paste_id để bỏ gói trễ sau Hủy."""
104:     parts = [p for p in (paths_str or "").split("|") if p]
105:     paste_id = None
106:     if parts and parts[0].isdigit():
107:         paste_id = int(parts[0])
108:         parts = parts[1:]
109:     return paste_id, parts

112: 
113: def create_named_pipe_with_everyone_dacl():
114:     """
115:     Tạo Named Pipe Server với Security Descriptor cho phép nhóm Everyone 
116:     có quyền Read/Write. TUYỆT ĐỐI KHÔNG truyền None vào Security Attributes.
117:     """
118:     import win32pipe
119:     import win32file
120:     import win32security
121:     import ntsecuritycon as con
122:     
123:     # Tạo Security Descriptor với DACL cho Everyone
124:     sd = win32security.SECURITY_DESCRIPTOR()
125:     sd.Initialize()
126:     
127:     # Tạo DACL
128:     dacl = win32security.ACL()
129:     dacl.Initialize()
130:     
131:     # Lấy SID của nhóm "Everyone"
132:     everyone_sid = win32security.CreateWellKnownSid(win32security.WinWorldSid)
133:     
134:     # Thêm quyền Read/Write cho Everyone
135:     dacl.AddAccessAllowedAce(
136:         win32security.ACL_REVISION,
137:         con.FILE_GENERIC_READ | con.FILE_GENERIC_WRITE,
138:         everyone_sid
139:     )
140:     
141:     sd.SetSecurityDescriptorDacl(True, dacl, False)
142:     
143:     # Tạo Security Attributes
144:     sa = win32security.SECURITY_ATTRIBUTES()
145:     sa.bInheritHandle = False
146:     sa.SECURITY_DESCRIPTOR = sd
147:     
148:     # Tạo Named Pipe
149:     pipe_handle = win32pipe.CreateNamedPipe(
150:         CLIPBOARD_PIPE_NAME,
151:         win32pipe.PIPE_ACCESS_OUTBOUND,                    # Server chỉ ghi (outbound)
152:         win32pipe.PIPE_TYPE_MESSAGE | win32pipe.PIPE_WAIT,  # Message mode, blocking
153:         4,       # Số instance tối đa
154:         10 * 1024 * 1024,    # Output buffer size (10MB)
155:         10 * 1024 * 1024,    # Input buffer size (10MB)
156:         0,       # Default timeout
157:         sa       # Security Attributes với DACL cho Everyone
158:     )
159:     
160:     return pipe_handle
161: 
162: 
163: def _low_il_everyone_sa():
164:     """SECURITY_ATTRIBUTES: Everyone + Mandatory Label Low — user Medium IL ghi được."""
165:     import win32security
166:     sa = win32security.SECURITY_ATTRIBUTES()
167:     sa.bInheritHandle = 0
168:     try:
169:         sd = win32security.ConvertStringSecurityDescriptorToSecurityDescriptor(
170:             "D:(A;;GA;;;WD)(A;;GA;;;AN)S:(ML;;NW;;;LW)",
171:             win32security.SDDL_REVISION_1,
172:         )
173:         sa.SECURITY_DESCRIPTOR = sd
174:     except Exception:
175:         sd = win32security.SECURITY_DESCRIPTOR()
176:         sd.Initialize()
177:         sd.SetSecurityDescriptorDacl(True, None, False)
178:         sa.SECURITY_DESCRIPTOR = sd
179:     return sa

216: def _install_paste_ll_hooks(on_ctrl_v, on_lbutton, on_rbutton):
217:     """Hook LL chỉ cho clipboard-agent (host). Callback phải PostMessage, không OpenClipboard."""
218:     user32 = ctypes.windll.user32
219:     kernel32 = ctypes.windll.kernel32
220:     kernel32.GetModuleHandleW.restype = ctypes.c_void_p
221:     user32.SetWindowsHookExW.restype = ctypes.c_void_p
222: 
223:     def _kb(nCode, wParam, lParam):
224:         try:
225:             if nCode >= 0 and int(wParam) in (0x0100, 0x0104):
226:                 kb = ctypes.cast(int(lParam), ctypes.POINTER(_KBDLLHOOKSTRUCT)).contents
227:                 vk = int(kb.vkCode)
228:                 ctrl = bool(user32.GetAsyncKeyState(0x11) & 0x8000)
229:                 shift = bool(user32.GetAsyncKeyState(0x10) & 0x8000)
230:                 if (vk in (0x56, 0x76) and ctrl) or (vk == 0x2D and shift):
231:                     on_ctrl_v()
232:         except Exception:
233:             pass
234:         return _CallNextHookEx(None, nCode, wParam, lParam)
235: 
236:     def _mouse(nCode, wParam, lParam):
237:         try:
238:             if nCode >= 0:
239:                 msg = int(wParam)
240:                 if msg in (0x0201, 0x0203):
241:                     on_lbutton()
242:                 elif msg in (0x0204, 0x0206):
243:                     on_rbutton()
244:         except Exception:
245:             pass
246:         return _CallNextHookEx(None, nCode, wParam, lParam)
247: 
248:     kb_proc = _HOOKPROC(_kb)
249:     mouse_proc = _HOOKPROC(_mouse)
250:     hmod = kernel32.GetModuleHandleW(None)
251:     hk_kb = user32.SetWindowsHookExW(13, kb_proc, hmod, 0)
252:     hk_mouse = user32.SetWindowsHookExW(14, mouse_proc, hmod, 0)
253:     _LL_HOOK_KEEPALIVE.extend((kb_proc, mouse_proc, hk_kb, hk_mouse))
254:     return hk_kb, hk_mouse

280:     def _handle_uppipe_client(self, pipe_handle):

503:     def _send_to_pipe(self, data_type, payload):
504:         """Gửi TYPE:payload xuống Clipboard Agent qua pipe giữ kết nối."""

516:     def _repush_pending_offer_to_agent(self, reason=""):
517:         """Gửi lại PENDING xuống agent nếu headless đang giữ offer client→host."""
518:         if not (self.app and getattr(self.app, "is_headless", False)):
519:             return

521:             getattr(self, "pending_remote_files", None)
522:             or getattr(self, "_reoffer_files", None)
523:             or []

526:             try:
527:                 log_overwrite_conflict(f"HOST_HEADLESS repush skip (no offer) reason={reason}")
528:             except Exception:
529:                 pass
530:             return

543:             log_overwrite_conflict(

545:                 f"n={len(files)} names={names}"
546:             )
547:         except Exception:
548:             pass

551:     def _ensure_down_pipe(self):
552:         if getattr(self, "_down_pipe_started", False):
553:             return
554:         self._down_pipe_started = True
555:         threading.Thread(target=self._down_pipe_loop, daemon=True, name="ClipDownPipe").start()
556: 
557:     def _down_pipe_loop(self):
558:         """Giữ 1 kết nối Named Pipe xuống agent — không tạo/đóng pipe mỗi lần PENDING (trễ 0.5–1.5s)."""
559:         while True:
560:             pipe_handle = None
561:             try:
562:                 pipe_handle = create_named_pipe_with_everyone_dacl()
563:                 if pipe_handle is None or pipe_handle == -1:
564:                     time.sleep(0.15)
565:                     continue
566:                 log_debug("[_down_pipe_loop] Đang chờ Clipboard Agent kết nối...")
567:                 try:
568:                     win32pipe.ConnectNamedPipe(pipe_handle, None)
569:                 except Exception as ce:
570:                     err = getattr(ce, "winerror", 0) or (ce.args[0] if ce.args else 0)
571:                     if err != 535:
572:                         raise
573:                 log_debug("[_down_pipe_loop] Agent đã kết nối. Giữ pipe để gửi PENDING/TEXT.")
574:                 try:
575:                     log_overwrite_conflict("HOST_HEADLESS DownPipe AGENT CONNECTED — flush PENDING if any")
576:                 except Exception:
577:                     pass
578:                 # Agent mới (sau cài/restart) — gửi lại offer đang giữ, tránh paste client→host mất PENDING.
579:                 try:
580:                     self._repush_pending_offer_to_agent("down_pipe_connected")
581:                 except Exception as re:
582:                     log_debug(f"[_down_pipe_loop] repush PENDING: {re}")
583:                 while True:
584:                     data_type, payload = self._down_pipe_q.get()
585:                     msg = f"{data_type}:{payload}\x00"
586:                     try:
587:                         win32file.WriteFile(pipe_handle, msg.encode("utf-8"))
588:                         if data_type == "PENDING":
589:                             try:
590:                                 log_overwrite_conflict(
591:                                     f"HOST_HEADLESS WriteFile PENDING OK ({len(msg)} bytes) → agent"
592:                                 )
593:                             except Exception:
594:                                 pass
595:                     except Exception as we:
596:                         log_debug(f"[_down_pipe_loop] WriteFile lỗi: {we}")
597:                         try:
598:                             log_overwrite_conflict(f"HOST_HEADLESS WriteFile {data_type} FAIL: {we}")
599:                         except Exception:
600:                             pass
601:                         try:
602:                             self._down_pipe_q.put((data_type, payload))
603:                         except Exception:
604:                             pass
605:                         break
606:             except Exception as e:
607:                 log_debug(f"[_down_pipe_loop] {e}")
608:             finally:
609:                 if pipe_handle is not None and pipe_handle != -1:
610:                     try:
611:                         win32file.FlushFileBuffers(pipe_handle)
612:                     except Exception:
613:                         pass
614:                     try:
615:                         win32pipe.DisconnectNamedPipe(pipe_handle)
616:                     except Exception:
617:                         pass
618:                     try:
619:                         win32file.CloseHandle(pipe_handle)
620:                     except Exception:
621:                         pass
622:             time.sleep(0.05)

630:     def _schedule_copied_files_sync(self):
631:         """Đọc file list sau khi seq clipboard ổn định; không gửi list cũ khi user đã copy file mới."""
632:         gen = getattr(self, "_clip_sync_gen", 0) + 1
633:         self._clip_sync_gen = gen
634:         old = getattr(self, "_clip_sync_timer", None)
635:         if old is not None:
636:             try:
637:                 old.cancel()
638:             except Exception:

640: 
641:         is_client = bool(getattr(self, "pygame_hwnd", None))
642: 
643:         def _pass(g=gen, tries=0):
644:             if g != getattr(self, "_clip_sync_gen", 0):
645:                 return
646:             seq_before = get_clipboard_sequence_number()
647:             # Client ưu tiên CIDA; nếu rỗng thì đọc HDROP (Win11 Explorer đôi khi thiếu Shell IDList).
648:             files = get_clipboard_files(retries=1, allow_hdrop=not is_client)
649:             if is_client and not files:
650:                 files = get_clipboard_files(retries=2, allow_hdrop=True)
651:             seq_after = get_clipboard_sequence_number()
652:             if seq_before and seq_after and seq_before != seq_after and tries < 4:
653:                 t = threading.Timer(0.12, lambda: _pass(g, tries + 1))
654:                 t.daemon = True
655:                 t.start()
656:                 self._clip_sync_timer = t
657:                 return
658:             if files:
659:                 fp = tuple(os.path.abspath(f).lower() for f in files)
660:                 last_fp = getattr(self, "_last_sent_file_fp", None)
661:                 last_seq = getattr(self, "_last_sent_file_seq", None)
662:                 # Trùng file + chưa đổi seq clipboard → không gửi lại (tránh offer 2,3,4 spam).
663:                 if last_fp and fp == last_fp and (not seq_after or seq_after == last_seq):
664:                     return
665:                 if last_fp and fp == last_fp and seq_after and seq_after != last_seq and tries < 4:
666:                     t = threading.Timer(0.12, lambda: _pass(g, tries + 1))
667:                     t.daemon = True
668:                     t.start()
669:                     self._clip_sync_timer = t
670:                     return
671:                 if is_client:
672:                     try:
673:                         log_overwrite_conflict(
674:                             f"CLIENT clip_sync files n={len(files)} "
675:                             f"names={[os.path.basename(f) for f in files[:8]]}"
676:                         )
677:                     except Exception:
678:                         pass
679:                 self._process_clipboard_change(provided_files=files, force=True)
680:                 return
681:             if is_client and tries < 2:
682:                 t = threading.Timer(0.18, lambda: _pass(g, tries + 1))
683:                 t.daemon = True
684:                 t.start()
685:                 self._clip_sync_timer = t
686:                 return
687:             if is_client:
688:                 try:
689:                     log_overwrite_conflict(
690:                         f"CLIENT clip_sync EMPTY after retries "
691:                         f"(khong gui files_copied_meta — Paste host se khong co PENDING)"
692:                     )
693:                 except Exception:
694:                     pass
695: 
696:         t = threading.Timer(0.22, lambda: _pass(gen, 0))
697:         t.daemon = True
698:         t.start()
699:         self._clip_sync_timer = t

105: def _pump_messages_except_clipboard_render():
106:     """Bơm message khi đang chờ tải file, nhưng không Dispatch WM_RENDERFORMAT.
107: 
108:     PeekMessage(hwnd=0) + Dispatch sẽ gọi lồng WM_RENDERFORMAT. Handler trùng
109:     return 0 không SetClipboardData → Explorer Win11 dán PE dở, không hiện dialog.
110:     Để RENDERFORMAT nằm lại queue, xử lý sau khi lần chờ hiện tại kết thúc.
111:     """
112:     user32 = ctypes.windll.user32
113:     msg = wintypes.MSG()
114:     PM_REMOVE = 1
115:     pumped = False
116:     while user32.PeekMessageW(ctypes.byref(msg), 0, 0, _WM_RENDERFORMAT - 1, PM_REMOVE):
117:         user32.TranslateMessage(ctypes.byref(msg))
118:         user32.DispatchMessageW(ctypes.byref(msg))
119:         pumped = True
120:     while user32.PeekMessageW(
121:         ctypes.byref(msg), 0, _WM_RENDERALLFORMATS + 1, 0xFFFFFFFF, PM_REMOVE
122:     ):
123:         user32.TranslateMessage(ctypes.byref(msg))
124:         user32.DispatchMessageW(ctypes.byref(msg))
125:         pumped = True
126:     return pumped

142: def _hide_win_path(path):
143:     """Ẩn thư mục tạm trên Windows (dấu chấm không ẩn Explorer)."""
144:     if sys.platform != "win32" or not path:
145:         return
146:     try:
147:         GetFileAttributesW = ctypes.windll.kernel32.GetFileAttributesW
148:         SetFileAttributesW = ctypes.windll.kernel32.SetFileAttributesW
149:         GetFileAttributesW.argtypes = [wintypes.LPCWSTR]
150:         GetFileAttributesW.restype = wintypes.DWORD
151:         SetFileAttributesW.argtypes = [wintypes.LPCWSTR, wintypes.DWORD]
152:         attrs = GetFileAttributesW(str(path))
153:         if attrs == 0xFFFFFFFF:
154:             return
155:         SetFileAttributesW(str(path), int(attrs) | 0x02 | 0x04)  # HIDDEN | SYSTEM
156:     except Exception:
157:         pass

163:     if dest_dir and xfer_id is not None:
164:         targets.append(_xfer_staging_dir(dest_dir, xfer_id))
165:     for p in extra_paths or []:
166:         if not p:
167:             continue
168:         try:
169:             ap = os.path.abspath(p)
170:             if os.path.basename(ap).startswith(".rdxfer_"):
171:                 targets.append(ap)
172:                 continue
173:             cur = ap if os.path.isdir(ap) else os.path.dirname(ap)
174:             for _ in range(10):
175:                 if not cur:
176:                     break
177:                 if os.path.basename(cur).startswith(".rdxfer_"):
178:                     targets.append(cur)
179:                     break
180:                 parent = os.path.dirname(cur)
181:                 if not parent or parent == cur:
182:                     break
183:                 cur = parent
184:         except Exception:
185:             pass
186:     if dest_dir and os.path.isdir(dest_dir):
187:         try:
188:             want = f".rdxfer_{int(xfer_id)}" if xfer_id is not None else None
189:             for name in os.listdir(dest_dir):
190:                 if not name.startswith(".rdxfer_"):
191:                     continue
192:                 p = os.path.join(dest_dir, name)
193:                 if force_all or (want and name == want):
194:                     targets.append(p)
195:                     continue
196:                 # Không đúng xfer này: chỉ xóa nếu rỗng (sót sau paste).
197:                 try:
198:                     if not os.listdir(p):
199:                         targets.append(p)
200:                 except Exception:
201:                     pass
202:         except Exception:
203:             pass
204:     seen = set()
205:     for t in targets:
206:         try:
207:             key = os.path.normcase(os.path.abspath(t))
208:         except Exception:
209:             continue
210:         if key in seen:
211:             continue
212:         seen.add(key)
213:         if not os.path.basename(t).startswith(".rdxfer_"):
214:             continue
215:         _retry_remove_path(t, retries=12)
216:         if os.path.lexists(t):
217:             try:
218:                 threading.Timer(0.6, lambda p=t: _retry_remove_path(p, retries=8)).start()
219:                 threading.Timer(2.0, lambda p=t: _retry_remove_path(p, retries=8)).start()
220:             except Exception:
221:                 pass
222: 
223: 
224: def _is_transfer_staging_dir(path):
225:     if not path:
226:         return False
227:     abs_path = os.path.normcase(os.path.abspath(path))
228:     staging = [
229:         os.path.normcase(os.path.abspath(HEADLESS_TRANSFER_DIR)),
230:         os.path.normcase(os.path.abspath(os.path.join(os.environ.get("TEMP", os.path.expanduser("~")), "RemoteDesktopTransfers"))),
231:     ]
232:     if abs_path in staging:
233:         return True
234:     base = os.path.basename(abs_path)
235:     return base.startswith(".rdxfer_")

238: def _file_offer_fp(files):
239:     """Vân tay danh sách file copy — để nhận copy mới dù offer_id bị lặp/reset."""
240:     parts = []
241:     for f in files or []:
242:         if isinstance(f, dict):
243:             name = os.path.basename(str(f.get("name") or "").replace("\\", "/"))
244:             path = os.path.normcase(os.path.abspath(str(f.get("path") or ""))) if f.get("path") else ""
245:             try:
246:                 size = int(f.get("size") or 0)
247:             except Exception:
248:                 size = 0
249:             parts.append((name, size, path))
250:         else:
251:             p = str(f or "")
252:             parts.append((os.path.basename(p.replace("\\", "/")), 0, os.path.normcase(os.path.abspath(p)) if p else ""))
253:     return tuple(parts)

256: def _rel_copy_name(item):
257:     if isinstance(item, dict):
258:         raw = str(item.get("name") or "")
259:     else:
260:         raw = str(item or "")
261:     return raw.replace("\\", "/").strip("/")

263: 
264: def _copy_batch_kind_and_name(files, fallback=""):
265:     """Paste file → ('file', tên file); paste thư mục → ('folder', tên folder)."""
266:     files = files or []
267:     names = []
268:     for item in files:
269:         name = _rel_copy_name(item)
270:         if name:
271:             names.append(name)
272:     if not names:
273:         raw = fallback or _("Tệp tin")
274:         return "file", os.path.basename(str(raw).replace("\\", "/")) or raw
275: 
276:     folder_tops = []
277:     seen_folders = set()
278:     loose = []
279:     for name in names:
280:         parts = [p for p in name.split("/") if p]
281:         if len(parts) >= 2:
282:             top = parts[0]
283:             if top not in seen_folders:
284:                 seen_folders.add(top)
285:                 folder_tops.append(top)
286:         else:
287:             loose.append(parts[0] if parts else name)
288: 
289:     if folder_tops and not loose:
290:         label = folder_tops[0]
291:         extra = len(folder_tops) - 1
292:         if extra > 0:
293:             label += _(" và {count} mục khác").format(count=extra)
294:         return "folder", label
295: 
296:     label = os.path.basename(names[0]) or names[0]
297:     extra = len(names) - 1
298:     if extra > 0:
299:         label += _(" và {count} mục khác").format(count=extra)
300:     return "file", label
301: 
302: 
303: def _localized_batch_name(files, fallback=""):
304:     _kind, name = _copy_batch_kind_and_name(files, fallback)
305:     return name

308: def _same_volume(path_a, path_b):
309:     try:
310:         return os.path.splitdrive(os.path.abspath(path_a))[0].lower() == os.path.splitdrive(os.path.abspath(path_b))[0].lower()
311:     except Exception:
312:         return False

314: 
315: def _path_byte_size(path):
316:     try:
317:         if os.path.isfile(path):
318:             return os.path.getsize(path)
319:         total = 0
320:         for root, _dirs, files in os.walk(path):
321:             for name in files:
322:                 try:
323:                     total += os.path.getsize(os.path.join(root, name))
324:                 except Exception:
325:                     pass
326:         return total
327:     except Exception:
328:         return 0

330: 
331: def _copy_file_with_progress(src, dst, on_progress=None, should_stop=None):
332:     import shutil
333:     wrote = 0
334:     completed = False
335:     try:
336:         with open(src, "rb") as inf, open(dst, "wb") as outf:
337:             while True:
338:                 if should_stop and should_stop():
339:                     raise InterruptedError("cancel")
340:                 buf = inf.read(1024 * 1024)
341:                 if not buf:
342:                     break
343:                 outf.write(buf)
344:                 wrote += len(buf)
345:                 if on_progress:
346:                     on_progress(len(buf))
347:         try:
348:             shutil.copystat(src, dst, follow_symlinks=True)
349:         except Exception:
350:             pass
351:         completed = True
352:         try:
353:             os.remove(src)
354:         except Exception:
355:             threading.Thread(target=_retry_remove_path, args=(src,), daemon=True).start()
356:     except Exception:
357:         if not completed:
358:             try:
359:                 _retry_remove_path(dst)
360:             except Exception:
361:                 pass
362:         raise
363: 
364: 
365: _file_io_lock = threading.Lock()
366: 
367: 
368: def relocate_transfer_files(src_paths, dest_dir, on_progress=None, should_stop=None):
369:     """Chuyển file từ thư mục tạm sang thư mục Explorer đang Paste. Trả về (paths, moved_all)."""
370:     with _file_io_lock:
371:         return _relocate_transfer_files_unlocked(src_paths, dest_dir, on_progress, should_stop)
372: 
373: 
374: def _relocate_transfer_files_unlocked(src_paths, dest_dir, on_progress=None, should_stop=None, replace_keys=None):
375:     """Chuyển file từ thư mục tạm sang thư mục Explorer đang Paste. Trả về (paths, moved_all).
376: 
377:     replace_keys: set đường dẫn đích (normcase abspath) được phép ghi đè.
378:     Nếu đích đã có file thật mà không nằm trong replace_keys → KHÔNG xóa/ghi đè.
379:     """
380:     import shutil
381:     if not dest_dir or not os.path.isdir(dest_dir) or _is_transfer_staging_dir(dest_dir):
382:         return list(src_paths or []), False
383:     allow_replace = set()
384:     for k in (replace_keys or []):
385:         try:
386:             allow_replace.add(os.path.normcase(os.path.abspath(k)))
387:         except Exception:
388:             allow_replace.add(k)
389:     dest_abs = os.path.normcase(os.path.abspath(dest_dir))
390:     moved = []
391:     all_in_dest = True
392:     for src in src_paths or []:
393:         if should_stop and should_stop():
394:             all_in_dest = False
395:             break
396:         if not src or not os.path.exists(src):
397:             continue
398:         src_abs = os.path.abspath(src)
399:         if os.path.basename(src_abs).startswith(".rdxfer_") or _is_transfer_staging_dir(src_abs):
400:             continue
401:         if _is_probe_stub_name(src_abs):
402:             continue
403:         src_dir = os.path.normcase(os.path.dirname(src_abs))
404:         if src_dir == dest_abs:
405:             moved.append(src_abs)
406:             if on_progress:
407:                 on_progress(_path_byte_size(src_abs))
408:             continue
409:         dest_path = os.path.join(dest_dir, os.path.basename(src_abs))
410:         try:
411:             dest_key = os.path.normcase(os.path.abspath(dest_path))
412:         except Exception:
413:             dest_key = dest_path
414:         try:
415:             if should_stop and should_stop():
416:                 raise InterruptedError("cancel")
417:             # Folder paste: đích đã có file chuyển sớm — gộp, không rmtree xóa file đã xong.
418:             if os.path.isdir(src_abs) and os.path.isdir(dest_path):
419:                 child_ok = True
420:                 try:
421:                     names = os.listdir(src_abs)
422:                 except Exception:
423:                     names = []
424:                 for name in names:
425:                     child = os.path.join(src_abs, name)
426:                     _nested, ok = _relocate_transfer_files_unlocked(
427:                         [child], dest_path, on_progress, should_stop, replace_keys=allow_replace
428:                     )
429:                     if not ok:
430:                         child_ok = False
431:                 if child_ok:
432:                     try:
433:                         os.rmdir(src_abs)
434:                     except Exception:
435:                         pass
436:                 else:
437:                     all_in_dest = False
438:                 moved.append(os.path.abspath(dest_path))
439:                 continue
440:             if os.path.exists(dest_path):
441:                 if not _is_transfer_junk_at_dest(dest_path) and dest_key not in allow_replace:
442:                     log_debug(f"[relocate] Bo ghi de (chua hoi Replace): {dest_path}")
443:                     # Giữ bản staging — caller (agent) sẽ relocate sau khi có replace_keys.
444:                     moved.append(src_abs)
445:                     all_in_dest = False
446:                     continue
447:                 if os.path.isdir(dest_path) and not os.path.islink(dest_path):
448:                     shutil.rmtree(dest_path, ignore_errors=True)
449:                 else:
450:                     os.remove(dest_path)
451:             if os.path.isdir(src_abs) or _same_volume(src_abs, dest_path):
452:                 shutil.move(src_abs, dest_path)
453:                 if on_progress:
454:                     on_progress(_path_byte_size(dest_path))
455:             else:
456:                 _copy_file_with_progress(src_abs, dest_path, on_progress, should_stop=should_stop)
457:             moved.append(os.path.abspath(dest_path))
458:         except InterruptedError:
459:             _retry_remove_path(dest_path)
460:             all_in_dest = False
461:             break
462:         except Exception as e:
463:             log_debug(f"[relocate_transfer_files] move failed {src_abs} -> {dest_path}: {e}")
464:             try:
465:                 if should_stop and should_stop():
466:                     raise InterruptedError("cancel")
467:                 if os.path.exists(dest_path) and not _is_transfer_junk_at_dest(dest_path) and dest_key not in allow_replace:
468:                     raise RuntimeError("dest exists, no replace")
469:                 if os.path.isdir(src_abs):
470:                     shutil.copytree(src_abs, dest_path)
471:                     shutil.rmtree(src_abs, ignore_errors=True)
472:                     if on_progress:
473:                         on_progress(_path_byte_size(dest_path))
474:                 else:
475:                     _copy_file_with_progress(src_abs, dest_path, on_progress, should_stop=should_stop)
476:                 moved.append(os.path.abspath(dest_path))
477:             except InterruptedError:
478:                 _retry_remove_path(dest_path)
479:                 all_in_dest = False
480:                 break
481:             except Exception as e2:
482:                 log_debug(f"[relocate_transfer_files] copy fallback failed: {e2}")
483:                 _retry_remove_path(dest_path)
484:                 moved.append(src_abs)
485:                 all_in_dest = False
486:     if moved and all_in_dest:
487:         for p in moved:
488:             if os.path.normcase(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(p))) != dest_abs:
489:                 all_in_dest = False
490:                 break
491:     return moved, bool(moved) and all_in_dest
492: 
493: 
494: def _promote_completed_xfer_file(staging_path, dest_dir, rel_name, replace_keys=None):
495:     """Đưa 1 file đã tải xong từ .rdxfer_* ra thư mục đích (giữ cây thư mục). Rename cùng ổ — không copy 4GB."""
496:     import shutil
497:     if not staging_path or not dest_dir or not os.path.isfile(staging_path):
498:         return staging_path
499:     if _is_transfer_staging_dir(dest_dir) or not os.path.isdir(dest_dir):
500:         return staging_path
501:     rel = str(rel_name or os.path.basename(staging_path)).replace("/", os.sep).replace("\\", os.sep)
502:     rel = rel.lstrip("\\/")
503:     if not rel:
504:         rel = os.path.basename(staging_path)
505:     dest_path = os.path.join(dest_dir, rel)
506:     try:
507:         if os.path.normcase(os.path.abspath(staging_path)) == os.path.normcase(os.path.abspath(dest_path)):
508:             return dest_path
509:     except Exception:
510:         pass
511:     parent = os.path.dirname(dest_path)
512:     if parent:
513:         os.makedirs(parent, exist_ok=True)
514:     allow_replace = set()
515:     for k in (replace_keys or []):
516:         try:
517:             allow_replace.add(os.path.normcase(os.path.abspath(k)))
518:         except Exception:
519:             allow_replace.add(k)
520:     if os.path.exists(dest_path):
521:         try:
522:             dest_key = os.path.normcase(os.path.abspath(dest_path))
523:         except Exception:
524:             dest_key = dest_path
525:         top_key = None
526:         try:
527:             top = rel.replace("\\", "/").strip("/").split("/")[0]
528:             top_key = os.path.normcase(os.path.abspath(os.path.join(dest_dir, top)))
529:         except Exception:
530:             pass
531:         may_replace = dest_key in allow_replace or (top_key and top_key in allow_replace)
532:         # Không im lặng ghi đè file user — chỉ xóa stub/junk; file thật cần replace_keys
533:         # (user đã chọn Replace trên ClassicCopyDialog).
534:         if not _is_transfer_junk_at_dest(dest_path) and not may_replace:
535:             log_debug(f"[promote] Giữ file đích đã có, không ghi đè: {dest_path}")
536:             try:
537:                 log_overwrite_conflict(
538:                     f"PROMOTE keep existing (no overwrite): {dest_path} "
539:                     f"staging={staging_path}"
540:                 )
541:             except Exception:
542:                 pass
543:             return staging_path
544:         try:
545:             log_overwrite_conflict(
546:                 f"PROMOTE remove junk then write: {dest_path}"
547:             )
548:             if os.path.isdir(dest_path) and not os.path.islink(dest_path):
549:                 shutil.rmtree(dest_path, ignore_errors=True)
550:             else:
551:                 os.remove(dest_path)
552:         except Exception:
553:             _retry_remove_path(dest_path)
554:     try:
555:         os.replace(staging_path, dest_path)
556:         try:
557:             log_overwrite_conflict(f"PROMOTE ok → {dest_path}")
558:         except Exception:
559:             pass
560:     except OSError:
561:         shutil.move(staging_path, dest_path)
562:         try:
563:             log_overwrite_conflict(f"PROMOTE move → {dest_path}")
564:         except Exception:
565:             pass
566:     return os.path.abspath(dest_path)

568: 
569: def _collect_actual_file_sizes(paths):
570:     """Map relative posix path → size. Folder paste: paths là thư mục top-level, không phải từng file."""
571:     actual = {}
572:     for p in paths:
573:         if not p or not os.path.exists(p):
574:             return None
575:         if os.path.isfile(p):
576:             try:
577:                 actual[os.path.basename(p).replace("\\", "/")] = os.path.getsize(p)
578:             except Exception:
579:                 return None
580:             continue
581:         if not os.path.isdir(p):
582:             return None
583:         root_parent = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(p))
584:         for dirpath, _dirs, files in os.walk(p):
585:             for fn in files:
586:                 full = os.path.join(dirpath, fn)
587:                 rel = os.path.relpath(full, root_parent).replace("\\", "/")
588:                 try:
589:                     actual[rel] = os.path.getsize(full)
590:                 except Exception:
591:                     return None
592:     return actual
593: 
594: 
595: def _paths_match_expected_sizes(paths, meta_files, detail=None):
596:     """False nếu thiếu file hoặc size không khớp metadata — tránh dán PE dở (mất icon).
597: 
598:     So khớp theo đường dẫn tương đối (thư mục/file), không chỉ basename — tránh
599:     trùng tên trong thư mục con và paste cả folder (paths là dir, không phải file).
600:     Nếu `detail` là list, append lý do thất bại.
601:     """
602:     def _fail(msg):
603:         if isinstance(detail, list):
604:             detail.append(msg)
605:         return False
606: 
607:     if not paths or not meta_files:
608:         return _fail("thieu paths hoac metadata")
609:     expected = {}
610:     for f in meta_files:
611:         if not isinstance(f, dict):
612:             continue
613:         name = str(f.get("name") or "").replace("\\", "/")
614:         if not name:
615:             continue
616:         try:
617:             expected[name] = int(f.get("size") or 0)
618:         except Exception:
619:             return _fail("metadata size khong hop le: %s" % (f.get("name"),))
620:     if not expected:
621:         return _fail("metadata khong co file")
622:     try:
623:         actual = _collect_actual_file_sizes(paths)
624:     except Exception as e:
625:         return _fail("khong doc duoc file da tai: %s" % e)
626:     if actual is None:
627:         return _fail("thieu file/thu muc tren disk (paths=%s)" % ([os.path.basename(p) for p in paths[:8]],))
628:     actual_l = {k.lower(): (k, v) for k, v in actual.items()}
629:     for name, size in expected.items():
630:         hit = actual_l.get(name.lower())
631:         if hit is None:
632:             base = name.split("/")[-1].lower()
633:             hits = [v for k, v in actual_l.items() if k.split("/")[-1] == base]
634:             if len(hits) == 1:
635:                 hit = hits[0]
636:             else:
637:                 return _fail("thieu %s (expected %s bytes)" % (name, size))
638:         got = hit[1]
639:         if got != size:
640:             return _fail("%s size %s != expected %s" % (name, got, size))
641:     return True
642: 
643: 
644: def _notify_explorer_paths_changed(paths):
645:     """Báo Explorer vẽ lại ngay sau khi app tự đặt file (tránh ding + phải đổi thư mục mới thấy)."""
646:     if not paths:
647:         return
648:     try:
649:         shell32 = ctypes.windll.shell32
650:         SHCNE_CREATE = 0x00000002
651:         SHCNE_UPDATEITEM = 0x00002000
652:         SHCNF_PATHW = 0x0005
653:         SHCNF_FLUSH = 0x1000
654:         for p in paths:
655:             if not p:
656:                 continue
657:             try:
658:                 ap = os.path.abspath(p)
659:             except Exception:
660:                 continue
661:             try:
662:                 shell32.SHChangeNotify(SHCNE_CREATE, SHCNF_PATHW | SHCNF_FLUSH, ap, None)
663:                 shell32.SHChangeNotify(SHCNE_UPDATEITEM, SHCNF_PATHW | SHCNF_FLUSH, ap, None)
664:             except Exception:
665:                 pass
666:             parent = os.path.dirname(ap)
667:             if parent:
668:                 try:
669:                     shell32.SHChangeNotify(SHCNE_UPDATEITEM, SHCNF_PATHW | SHCNF_FLUSH, parent, None)
670:                 except Exception:
671:                     pass
672:     except Exception as e:
673:         log_debug(f"[_notify_explorer_paths_changed] {e}")
674: 
675: 
676: def query_explorer_folder_path(allow_open_clipboard=False):
677:     """Thư mục Explorer/Desktop đang focus — gọi từ background thread (không gọi trong WM_RENDERFORMAT)."""
678:     import win32gui
679:     import win32process

3609:     def _enqueue_file_offer(self, files, offer_id=None):
3610:         """Hàng đợi copy file (RAM). Áp từng offer; nếu xếp hàng thì lấy cái mới nhất."""
3611:         if not files:
3612:             return
3613:         snap = []
3614:         for f in files:
3615:             snap.append(dict(f) if isinstance(f, dict) else f)
3616:         oid = 0
3617:         try:
3618:             oid = int(offer_id or 0)
3619:         except Exception:
3620:             oid = 0
3621:         with self._clip_offer_lock:
3622:             if oid <= 0:
3623:                 self._clip_offer_in = int(getattr(self, "_clip_offer_in", 0) or 0) + 1
3624:                 oid = self._clip_offer_in
3625:             else:
3626:                 self._clip_offer_in = max(int(getattr(self, "_clip_offer_in", 0) or 0), oid)
3627:             q = self._clip_offer_q
3628:             last_fp = getattr(self, "_clip_offer_fp", None)
3629:             fp = _file_offer_fp(snap)
3630:             applied = int(getattr(self, "_clip_offer_applied", 0) or 0)
3631:             if oid and oid <= applied and fp == last_fp:
3632:                 log_debug(f"[_enqueue_file_offer] Bỏ offer trùng {oid} (đã áp {applied}).")
3633:                 return
3634:             if oid and oid <= applied and fp != last_fp:
3635:                 oid = applied + 1
3636:                 self._clip_offer_in = oid
3637:                 print(f"[Clipboard] Offer id cũ nhưng file mới — nhận copy mới oid={oid}")
3638:             self._clip_offer_fp = fp
3639:             q.append({"offer_id": oid, "files": snap})
3640:             while len(q) > 24:
3641:                 q.pop(0)
3642:         names = []
3643:         for f in snap[:6]:
3644:             if isinstance(f, dict):
3645:                 names.append(f.get("name") or os.path.basename(str(f.get("path") or "")))
3646:         print(f"[Clipboard] Queue offer={oid} n={len(snap)} {names} (q={len(self._clip_offer_q)})")
3647:         self._clip_offer_evt.set()

3649:     def _clip_offer_loop(self):
3650:         while True:
3651:             self._clip_offer_evt.wait(timeout=1.0)
3652:             self._clip_offer_evt.clear()
3653:             while True:
3654:                 with self._clip_offer_lock:
3655:                     q = self._clip_offer_q
3656:                     if not q:
3657:                         break
3658:                     if len(q) == 1:
3659:                         item = q.pop(0)
3660:                     else:
3661:                         # Copy dồn: bỏ offer giữa, áp file mới nhất.
3662:                         item = q[-1]
3663:                         q.clear()
3664:                 self._apply_file_offer(item)
3665:                 time.sleep(0.06)

3667:     def _apply_file_offer(self, item):
3668:         oid = int((item or {}).get("offer_id") or 0)
3669:         files = list((item or {}).get("files") or [])
3670:         if not files:
3671:             return

3673:             applied = int(getattr(self, "_clip_offer_applied", 0) or 0)

3676:             if oid and oid < applied and fp == last_fp:
3677:                 return

3731:     def _paste_dest_for_job(self, job):
3732:         """Chỉ lấy dest đã gắn vào job / paste_id — không đọc cached_explorer_path sống."""
3733:         dest = None
3734:         if job:
3735:             dest = job.get("paste_dest")
3736:         if not dest:
3737:             try:
3738:                 pid = int((job or {}).get("paste_id") or 0)
3739:             except Exception:
3740:                 pid = 0
3741:             if pid:
3742:                 dest = (getattr(self, "_xfer_dest_by_paste_id", None) or {}).get(pid)
3743:         if not dest:
3744:             # Chỉ dùng _paste_dest_dir khi đang frozen (đang paste), không dùng path cũ lệch.
3745:             if getattr(self, "_paste_dest_frozen", False):
3746:                 dest = getattr(self, "_paste_dest_dir", None)
3747:         if dest and os.path.isdir(dest) and not _is_transfer_staging_dir(dest):
3748:             return dest
3749:         return None

3751:     def _ensure_dest_tree(self, dest, rel_name):
3752:         if not dest or not rel_name:
3753:             return
3754:         rel = str(rel_name).replace("\\", "/").strip("/")
3755:         top = rel.split("/")[0] if "/" in rel else None
3756:         if top:
3757:             try:
3758:                 os.makedirs(os.path.join(dest, top), exist_ok=True)
3759:             except Exception:
3760:                 pass

3762:     def _finalize_incoming_file(self, transfer, job, filename):
3763:         """close + kiểm size + đưa file ra thư mục đích. Chạy ngoài ClipPkt (tránh kẹt AV/close 4GB)."""
3764:         path = transfer.get("path")
3765:         expected = transfer.get("expected_size")
3766:         written = int(transfer.get("written") or 0)
3767:         handle = transfer.get("handle")
3768:         if handle is not None:
3769:             try:
3770:                 handle.flush()
3771:             except Exception:
3772:                 pass
3773:             try:
3774:                 handle.close()
3775:             except Exception as e:
3776:                 print(f"[FileTransfer] Lỗi đóng handle {filename}: {e}")
3777:         actual = -1
3778:         if path:
3779:             try:
3780:                 actual = os.path.getsize(path) if os.path.exists(path) else -1
3781:             except Exception:
3782:                 actual = -1
3783:         if expected is not None and actual != expected and written != int(expected or 0):
3784:             print(f"[FileTransfer] Sai kich thuoc {filename}: disk={actual} written={written} expected={expected}, xoa file.")
3785:             try:
3786:                 if path and os.path.exists(path):
3787:                     os.remove(path)
3788:             except Exception:
3789:                 pass
3790:             return
3791:         dest = self._paste_dest_for_job(job)
3792:         final_path = path
3793:         if dest and path and os.path.isfile(path):
3794:             try:

1778:     """File dở trong thư mục tạm .rdxfer_* — không dùng cho file sẵn có của user."""
1779:     if not path or not os.path.isfile(path):
1780:         return False
1781:     try:
1782:         sz = os.path.getsize(path)
1783:     except Exception:
1784:         return True
1785:     if expected is None:
1786:         return sz == 0
1787:     try:
1788:         expected = int(expected)
1789:     except Exception:
1790:         return sz == 0
1791:     if expected <= 0:
1792:         return False
1793:     return sz < expected

147: _WM_RENDERALLFORMATS = 0x0306
148: 
149: # Định nghĩa các kiểu dữ liệu tương thích 64-bit để tránh lỗi OverflowError trên Windows 64-bit
150: WPARAM_64 = ctypes.c_size_t
151: LPARAM_64 = ctypes.c_ssize_t
152: LRESULT_64 = ctypes.c_ssize_t
153: 
154: try:
155:     WNDPROCTYPE = ctypes.WINFUNCTYPE(LRESULT_64, ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_uint, WPARAM_64, LPARAM_64)
156:     class WNDCLASSEX(ctypes.Structure):
157:         _fields_ = [("cbSize", ctypes.c_uint), ("style", ctypes.c_uint), ("lpfnWndProc", WNDPROCTYPE),
158:                     ("cbClsExtra", ctypes.c_int), ("cbWndExtra", ctypes.c_int),
159:                     ("hInstance", ctypes.c_void_p), ("hIcon", ctypes.c_void_p),
160:                     ("hCursor", ctypes.c_void_p), ("hbrBackground", ctypes.c_void_p),
161:                     ("lpszMenuName", wintypes.LPCWSTR), ("lpszClassName", wintypes.LPCWSTR),
162:                     ("hIconSm", ctypes.c_void_p)]
163: except:
164:     pass
165: 
166: # Khởi tạo trước thông số kiểu dữ liệu của DefWindowProcW để tránh lỗi trong quá trình tạo cửa sổ
167: try:
168:     ctypes.windll.user32.DefWindowProcW.argtypes = [ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_uint, WPARAM_64, LPARAM_64]
169:     ctypes.windll.user32.DefWindowProcW.restype = LRESULT_64
170: except:
171:     pass
172: 
173: class ClipboardEventListener:
174:     def __init__(self, callback, manager=None):
175:         self.callback = callback
176:         self.manager = manager
177:         self.hwnd = None
178:         self.running = True
179:         self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self._run, daemon=True)
180:         self.thread.start()
181:         self.poll_thread = threading.Thread(target=self._mouse_poll_loop, daemon=True)
182:         self.poll_thread.start()
183: 
184:     def _mouse_poll_loop(self):
185:         user32 = ctypes.windll.user32
186:         while self.running:
187:             if user32.GetAsyncKeyState(0x01) & 0x8000:
188:                 if self.manager:
189:                     now = time.time()
190:                     was = getattr(self.manager, "_lmb_held", False)
191:                     self.manager.last_lbutton_time = now
192:                     on_self = _cursor_over_current_process()
193:                     hit_menu = False
194:                     if not was:
195:                         prev_t = getattr(self.manager, "_prev_lbutton_time", 0.0)
196:                         px, py = getattr(self.manager, "_prev_lbutton_pos", (0, 0))
197:                         cx, cy = _cursor_xy()
198:                         if (now - prev_t) <= (_double_click_ms() / 1000.0) + 0.05 and abs(cx - px) <= 6 and abs(cy - py) <= 6:
199:                             self.manager.last_dblclick_time = now
200:                         self.manager._prev_lbutton_time = now
201:                         self.manager._prev_lbutton_pos = (cx, cy)
202:                         hit_menu = (not on_self) and (not getattr(self.manager, "_rmb_is_taskbar", False)) and (not _is_taskbar_or_taskmgr_at_point((cx, cy))) and _cursor_over_context_menu()
203:                         paste_from_menu = False
204:                         last_r = float(getattr(self.manager, "last_rbutton_time", 0) or 0)
205:                         age_r = now - last_r
206:                         if hit_menu:
207:                             self.manager._lmb_hit_context_menu = True
208:                             kind, name = _capture_menu_item_at_cursor(rmb_pos=getattr(self.manager, "_rmb_cursor_pos", None))
209:                             self.manager._lmb_menu_item_kind = kind
210:                             # Win10: chữ Paste nếu đọc được; unknown trên #32768 sau RMB vẫn là RMP.
211:                             # Win11: click trên context menu sau RMB (0.12 < age_r < 8.0) và kind != "not_paste" là Paste.
212:                             if _menu_item_counts_as_paste(kind) or (
213:                                 kind != "not_paste"
214:                                 and 0.12 < age_r < 8.0
215:                             ):
216:                                 paste_from_menu = True
217:                                 try:
218:                                     from utils.logger import clip_trace
219:                                     clip_trace(
220:                                         f"[LMB menu] profile={_shell_menu_profile()} "
221:                                         f"kind={kind!r} name={name!r} → paste gesture"
222:                                     )
223:                                 except Exception:
224:                                     pass
225:                             else:
226:                                 try:
227:                                     from utils.logger import clip_trace
228:                                     clip_trace(
229:                                         f"[LMB menu] profile={_shell_menu_profile()} "
230:                                         f"kind={kind!r} name={name!r} → not paste"
231:                                     )
232:                                 except Exception:
233:                                     pass
234:                         else:
235:                             # Win11 XAML: hit test menu hay fail do XAML island window
236:                             chk_kind, chk_name = _capture_menu_item_at_cursor(rmb_pos=getattr(self.manager, "_rmb_cursor_pos", None))
237:                             rpos = getattr(self.manager, "_rmb_cursor_pos", None)
238:                             near_rmb = bool(
239:                                 rpos
240:                                 and not getattr(self.manager, "_rmb_is_taskbar", False)
241:                                 and abs(cx - rpos[0]) <= 360
242:                                 and abs(cy - rpos[1]) <= 360
243:                             )
244:                             recent_dbl = bool(getattr(self.manager, "last_dblclick_time", 0.0) and (now - float(getattr(self.manager, "last_dblclick_time", 0.0))) < 0.7)
245:                             menu_open = _context_menu_open()
246:                             if chk_kind == "paste":
247:                                 paste_from_menu = True
248:                                 try:
249:                                     from utils.logger import clip_trace
250:                                     clip_trace("[LMB menu] Win11 exact 'paste' hit -> paste gesture")
251:                                 except Exception:
252:                                     pass
253:                             elif chk_kind == "not_paste" and chk_name and chk_name not in ("[xaml-not-paste]", "[xaml-pos-not_paste]", "[show-more-strip]", ""):
254:                                 self.manager._lmb_hit_context_menu = False
255:                                 self.manager._lmb_menu_item_kind = "not_paste"
256:                             elif 0.12 < age_r < 8.0 and not recent_dbl and (menu_open or near_rmb) and not getattr(self.manager, "_rmb_is_taskbar", False):
257:                                 paste_from_menu = True
258:                                 self.manager._lmb_hit_context_menu = True
259:                                 self.manager._lmb_menu_item_kind = "paste"
260:                                 try:
261:                                     from utils.logger import clip_trace
262:                                     clip_trace(f"[LMB menu] Win11 RMB vicinity paste gesture (near_rmb={near_rmb}, menu_open={menu_open})")
263:                                 except Exception:
264:                                     pass
265:                             else:
266:                                 self.manager._lmb_hit_context_menu = False
267:                                 self.manager._lmb_menu_item_kind = "unknown"
268: 
269:                         if paste_from_menu:
270:                             if getattr(self.manager, "_conflict_ui_active", False):
271:                                 try:
272:                                     from utils.logger import clip_trace
273:                                     clip_trace("[LMB menu] ignore paste gesture — conflict dialog open")
274:                                 except Exception:
275:                                     pass
276:                             else:
277:                                 self.manager._lmb_hit_context_menu = True
278:                                 self.manager._lmb_menu_item_kind = "paste"
279:                                 self.manager.note_paste_gesture()
280:                                 self.manager.snapshot_paste_dest("menu_paste")
281:                                 try:
282:                                     self.manager._ensure_delayed_for_gesture()
283:                                 except Exception:
284:                                     pass
285:                     self.manager._lmb_held = True
286:                     # Không EmptyClipboard / rearm delayed khi LMB thường — gây vòng BACKGROUND.
287:             else:
288:                 if self.manager:
289:                     self.manager._lmb_held = False
290:             if user32.GetAsyncKeyState(0x02) & 0x8000:
291:                 if self.manager:
292:                     was_rmb = getattr(self.manager, "_rmb_held", False)
293:                     self.manager.last_rbutton_time = time.time()
294:                     self.manager._lmb_hit_context_menu = False
295:                     self.manager._lmb_menu_item_kind = ""
296:                     self.manager._rmb_held = True
297:                     if not was_rmb:
298:                         cxy = None
299:                         try:
300:                             cxy = _cursor_xy()
301:                         except Exception:
302:                             cxy = None
303:                         if cxy and _is_taskbar_or_taskmgr_at_point(cxy):
304:                             self.manager._rmb_is_taskbar = True
305:                             self.manager._rmb_cursor_pos = None
306:                         else:
307:                             self.manager._rmb_is_taskbar = False
308:                             self.manager._rmb_cursor_pos = cxy
309:                             if cxy:
310:                                 set_global_rmb_pos(cxy)
311:                             # Chốt path sync trước khi Explorer mở clipboard vẽ menu Paste.
312:                             self.manager.snapshot_paste_dest("rmb", sync=True)
313:                         pend = getattr(self.manager, "pending_remote_files", None) or []
314:                         try:
315:                             from utils.logger import clip_trace
316:                             clip_trace(
317:                                 f"[RMB] down pending_n={len(pend)} "
318:                                 f"dummy_h={getattr(self.manager,'dummy_h_active',False)} "
319:                                 f"profile={_shell_menu_profile()} "
320:                                 f"snap={getattr(self.manager,'_paste_dest_snapshot',None)}"
321:                             )
322:                         except Exception:
323:                             pass
324:                         # Menu sắp mở — cần delayed CF_HDROP để Explorer bật Paste.
325:                         if pend or getattr(self.manager, "_hdrop_materialized", False) or getattr(
326:                             self.manager, "dummy_h_active", False
327:                         ):
328:                             try:
329:                                 self.manager._ensure_delayed_for_gesture()
330:                             except Exception:
331:                                 pass
332:             else:
333:                 if self.manager:
334:                     self.manager._rmb_held = False
335:             # Poll nhanh hơn khi đang chờ Paste menu (Win11 race GetData).
336:             _sleep = 0.05
337:             try:
338:                 if self.manager and (
339:                     (time.time() - float(getattr(self.manager, "last_rbutton_time", 0) or 0)) < 8.0
340:                 ):
341:                     _sleep = 0.015
342:                     try:
343:                         _update_cached_menu_rects()
344:                     except Exception:
345:                         pass
346:             except Exception:
347:                 pass
348:             if user32.GetAsyncKeyState(0x0D) & 0x8000: # Enter
349:                 if self.manager and not getattr(self.manager, "_enter_held", False):
350:                     self.manager._enter_held = True
351:                     if getattr(self.manager, "dummy_h_active", False) or getattr(
352:                         self.manager, "_hdrop_materialized", False
353:                     ):
354:                         try:
355:                             self.manager._ensure_delayed_for_gesture()
356:                         except Exception:
357:                             pass
358:             else:
359:                 if self.manager:
360:                     self.manager._enter_held = False
361:             if user32.GetAsyncKeyState(0x1B) & 0x8000: # Esc
362:                 pass  # đừng EmptyClipboard vì Esc — tránh vòng probe
363:             if (user32.GetAsyncKeyState(0x11) & 0x8000) and (user32.GetAsyncKeyState(0x56) & 0x8000):
364:                 if self.manager:
365:                     if not getattr(self.manager, "_ctrl_v_held", False):
366:                         has_text = bool(user32.IsClipboardFormatAvailable(13) or user32.IsClipboardFormatAvailable(1))
367:                         is_exp = _is_explorer_clipboard_client()
368:                         if is_exp and not has_text:
369:                             self.manager.last_ctrl_v_time = time.time()
370:                             self.manager.note_paste_gesture()
371:                             self.manager.snapshot_paste_dest("ctrl_v")
372:                             try:
373:                                 self.manager._ensure_delayed_for_gesture()
374:                             except Exception:
375:                                 pass
376:                     self.manager._ctrl_v_held = True
377:             else:
378:                 if self.manager:
379:                     self.manager._ctrl_v_held = False
380:             if (user32.GetAsyncKeyState(0x10) & 0x8000) and (user32.GetAsyncKeyState(0x2D) & 0x8000):
381:                 if self.manager:
382:                     if not getattr(self.manager, "_shift_ins_held", False):
383:                         has_text = bool(user32.IsClipboardFormatAvailable(13) or user32.IsClipboardFormatAvailable(1))
384:                         is_exp = _is_explorer_clipboard_client()
385:                         if is_exp and not has_text:
386:                             self.manager.last_ctrl_v_time = time.time()
387:                             self.manager.note_paste_gesture()
388:                             self.manager.snapshot_paste_dest("shift_ins")
389:                             try:
390:                                 self.manager._ensure_delayed_for_gesture()
391:                             except Exception:
392:                                 pass
393:                     self.manager._shift_ins_held = True
394:             else:
395:                 if self.manager:
396:                     self.manager._shift_ins_held = False
397:             # Cùng file: gửi lại metadata đã cache — không GetClipboardData (Explorer chuột xoay).
398:             if (user32.GetAsyncKeyState(0x11) & 0x8000) and (user32.GetAsyncKeyState(0x43) & 0x8000):
399:                 if self.manager:
400:                     now = time.time()
401:                     if now - getattr(self.manager, "_last_ctrl_c_time", 0) > 0.4:
402:                         self.manager._last_ctrl_c_time = now
403:                         m = self.manager
404:                         seq = get_clipboard_sequence_number()
405:                         last_seq = getattr(m, "_last_sent_file_seq", None)
406:                         cached = getattr(m, "last_current_files", None)
407:                         if cached and seq and seq == last_seq:
408:                             threading.Thread(
409:                                 target=lambda: m._process_clipboard_change(
410:                                     provided_files=list(cached), force=True
411:                                 ),
412:                                 daemon=True,
413:                             ).start()
414:             time.sleep(_sleep)
415: 
416:     def _wndproc(self, hwnd, msg, wparam, lparam):
417:         WM_CLIPBOARDUPDATE = 0x031D
418:         WM_RENDERFORMAT = 0x0305
419:         WM_DESTROYCLIPBOARD = 0x0307
420:         WM_SETUP_DELAYED_RENDERING = 0x0400 + 101
421:         WM_SET_CLIP_TEXT = 0x0400 + 102
422:         
423:         if msg == WM_CLIPBOARDUPDATE:
424:             log_debug(f"[WndProc] Nhận WM_CLIPBOARDUPDATE")
425:             try:
426:                 time.sleep(0.05)
427:                 u32 = ctypes.windll.user32
428:                 u32.IsClipboardFormatAvailable.restype = ctypes.c_bool
429:                 has_img = u32.IsClipboardFormatAvailable(2) or u32.IsClipboardFormatAvailable(8) or u32.IsClipboardFormatAvailable(17)
430:                 has_txt = u32.IsClipboardFormatAvailable(13) or u32.IsClipboardFormatAvailable(1)
431:                 
432:                 from utils.clipboard_api import clipboard_has_file_formats
433:                 has_file = clipboard_has_file_formats()
434:                 
435:                 if has_img and not has_file and not has_txt:
436:                     log_debug("[Listener] Clipboard chỉ chứa Image, bỏ qua để tránh gây lỗi Zalo/WeChat.")
437:                     return 0
438:             except Exception as e:
439:                 log_debug(f"[Listener] Lỗi check image clipboard: {e}")
440:                 
441:             self.callback()
442:             return 0
443:         elif msg == WM_RENDERFORMAT:
444:             log_debug(f"[WndProc] Nhận WM_RENDERFORMAT. wparam={wparam}")
445:             if wparam == 15: # CF_HDROP
446:                 if self.manager:
447:                     self.manager.render_format(15)
448:                 return 0
449:         elif msg == WM_DESTROYCLIPBOARD:
450:             log_debug(f"[WndProc] Nhận WM_DESTROYCLIPBOARD")
451:             if self.manager:
452:                 self.manager.lost_ownership()
453:             return 0
454:         elif msg == WM_SETUP_DELAYED_RENDERING:
455:             log_debug(f"[WndProc] Nhận WM_SETUP_DELAYED_RENDERING. Đang tiến hành thiết lập delayed rendering...")
456:             if self.manager:
457:                 self.manager._execute_setup_delayed_rendering()
458:             return 0
459:         elif msg == WM_SET_CLIP_TEXT:
460:             if self.manager:
461:                 self.manager.apply_pending_clip_text_on_listener(hwnd)
462:             return 0
463:             
464:         try:
465:             return ctypes.windll.user32.DefWindowProcW(hwnd, msg, wparam, lparam)
466:         except:
467:             return 0
468: 
469:     def _run(self):
470:         try:
471:             log_debug("[Listener] Bắt đầu thread đăng ký Clipboard listener.")
472:             user32 = ctypes.windll.user32
473:             kernel32 = ctypes.windll.kernel32
474:             
475:             # Định nghĩa types cho GetModuleHandleW trước khi gọi
476:             kernel32.GetModuleHandleW.restype = ctypes.c_void_p
477:             h_mod = kernel32.GetModuleHandleW(None)
478:             
479:             # Không còn dùng Low-level Mouse Hook (WH_MOUSE_LL) để tránh lag chuột toàn hệ thống
480: 
481:             user32.CreateWindowExW.argtypes = [
482:                 ctypes.c_uint, wintypes.LPCWSTR, wintypes.LPCWSTR,
483:                 ctypes.c_uint, ctypes.c_int, ctypes.c_int, ctypes.c_int, ctypes.c_int,
484:                 ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_void_p
485:             ]
486:             user32.CreateWindowExW.restype = ctypes.c_void_p
487:             kernel32.GetModuleHandleW.restype = ctypes.c_void_p
488: 
489:             wndproc = WNDPROCTYPE(self._wndproc)
490:             self.wndproc_ref = wndproc  # Giữ reference để tránh bị garbage collected
491:             wndclass = WNDCLASSEX()
492:             wndclass.cbSize = ctypes.sizeof(WNDCLASSEX)
493:             wndclass.lpfnWndProc = wndproc
494:             wndclass.lpszClassName = "HiddenClipboardListener"
495:             wndclass.hInstance = kernel32.GetModuleHandleW(None)
496:             
497:             reg_res = user32.RegisterClassExW(ctypes.byref(wndclass))
498:             log_debug(f"[Listener] RegisterClassExW trả về: {reg_res}")
499:             
500:             # Cửa sổ ẩn thật (không HWND_MESSAGE): message-only thường không nhận WM_CLIPBOARDUPDATE.
501:             self.hwnd = user32.CreateWindowExW(0, wndclass.lpszClassName, "HiddenWindow", 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, None, None, wndclass.hInstance, None)
502:             log_debug(f"[Listener] CreateWindowExW trả về HWND: {self.hwnd}")
503:             
504:             try:
505:                 WM_CLIPBOARDUPDATE = 0x031D
506:                 WM_RENDERFORMAT = 0x0305
507:                 WM_DESTROYCLIPBOARD = 0x0307
508:                 MSGFLT_ALLOW = 1
509:                 user32.ChangeWindowMessageFilterEx(ctypes.c_void_p(self.hwnd), WM_CLIPBOARDUPDATE, MSGFLT_ALLOW, None)
510:                 user32.ChangeWindowMessageFilterEx(ctypes.c_void_p(self.hwnd), WM_RENDERFORMAT, MSGFLT_ALLOW, None)
511:                 user32.ChangeWindowMessageFilterEx(ctypes.c_void_p(self.hwnd), WM_DESTROYCLIPBOARD, MSGFLT_ALLOW, None)
512:                 log_debug("[Listener] ChangeWindowMessageFilterEx thành công.")
513:             except Exception as e:
514:                 log_debug(f"[Listener] ChangeWindowMessageFilterEx thất bại: {e}")
515: 
516:             add_res = user32.AddClipboardFormatListener(ctypes.c_void_p(self.hwnd))
517:             log_debug(f"[Listener] AddClipboardFormatListener trả về: {add_res}")
518:             
519:             EVENT_SYSTEM_FOREGROUND = 0x0003
520:             WINEVENT_OUTOFCONTEXT = 0x0000
521:             WINEVENTPROC = ctypes.WINFUNCTYPE(
522:                 None, wintypes.HANDLE, wintypes.DWORD, wintypes.HWND,
523:                 wintypes.LONG, wintypes.LONG, wintypes.DWORD, wintypes.DWORD
524:             )
525:             
526:             def wineventproc(hWinEventHook, event, hwnd, idObject, idChild, dwEventThread, dwmsEventTime):
527:                 if event == EVENT_SYSTEM_FOREGROUND:
528:                     if self.manager and hasattr(self.manager, 'on_foreground_changed'):
529:                         self.manager.on_foreground_changed(hwnd)
530: 
531:             self.wineventproc_c = WINEVENTPROC(wineventproc)
532:             hook = user32.SetWinEventHook(
533:                 EVENT_SYSTEM_FOREGROUND, EVENT_SYSTEM_FOREGROUND,
534:                 None, self.wineventproc_c, 0, 0, WINEVENT_OUTOFCONTEXT
535:             )
536:             # Không cài WH_KEYBOARD_LL / WH_MOUSE_LL trên viewer/client:
537:             # hook nằm cùng thread GetMessage (OpenClipboard/RENDERFORMAT) sẽ treo chuột+phím
538:             # cả máy local (vd. F2 đổi tên trong Explorer). Agent host vẫn cài LL hook riêng.
539: 
540:             msg = wintypes.MSG()
541:             while self.running and user32.GetMessageW(ctypes.byref(msg), 0, 0, 0) > 0:
542:                 user32.TranslateMessage(ctypes.byref(msg))
543:                 user32.DispatchMessageW(ctypes.byref(msg))
544:                 
545:             user32.RemoveClipboardFormatListener(ctypes.c_void_p(self.hwnd))
546:             user32.DestroyWindow(ctypes.c_void_p(self.hwnd))
547:             user32.UnregisterClassW(wndclass.lpszClassName, wndclass.hInstance)
548:         except Exception as e:
549:             print("[ClipboardEvent] Lỗi Listener:", e)
550: 
551:     def stop(self):
552:         self.running = False
553:         if self.hwnd:
554:             try: ctypes.windll.user32.PostMessageW(ctypes.c_void_p(self.hwnd), 0, 0, 0)
555:             except: pass
556: 
557: # Đường dẫn thư mục lưu file chuyển từ Client (dùng cho headless/SYSTEM mode)
558: 
559: class ClipboardSyncManager(ClipboardTextMixin, FileCopyClientMixin, FileCopyHostMixin):
560:     def __init__(self):
561:         import queue
562:         self.gui_queue = queue.Queue()
563:         self.last_files = []
564:         self.last_files_time = 0
565:         self.active_sockets = set()
566:         self.transfer_in_progress = False
567:         self.lock = threading.Lock()
568:         self.transfer_done_event = threading.Event()
569:         self.overwrite_event = threading.Event()
570:         self.overwrite_choice = None

696:     def enqueue_packet(self, packet):
697:         """Nhận packet clipboard/file ngoài luồng input — tránh kẹt chuột viewer khi ghi pipe/file."""

703:     def _packet_loop(self):
704:         while True:
705:             try:
706:                 packet = self._pkt_queue.get()
707:             except Exception:
708:                 continue
709:             if packet is None:
710:                 break
711:             try:
712:                 self.handle_received_packet(packet)
713:             except Exception as e:
714:                 print(f"[Clipboard] Packet worker: {e}")

944:     def remove_socket(self, sock):
945:         if not ENABLE_CLIPBOARD_SYNC: return
946:         with self.lock:
947:             if sock in self.active_sockets:
948:                 self.active_sockets.remove(sock)
949:             if self.sock == sock:
